# Dev Log — TripPilot Implementation

## Current State
- **🟢 0.99.36 (web/OTA + WORKER `13e6bdd9`) — Telemetria: contar só quem configurou o app + purge-all no admin.** Source: descoberta ao vivo logo após o deploy do 0.99.35 — o `/admin/overview` saltou p/ dezenas de installs em segundos, mas a tabela mostrou que TODAS (menos os 3 seeds de demo) eram `"Meu dispositivo"` com contadores zerados: o heartbeat estava disparando para **visitas vazias** (bots/crawlers/convidado abrindo link compartilhado / load sem onboarding), cada uma gerando um `installId` novo → inflando o painel com não-usuários. **Fix (cliente):** `sendHeartbeatIfDue` agora **só envia quando `settings.onboardingCompleted === true`** (além de respeitar opt-out e pular demo) — uma instalação nunca-configurada tem só o settings default transitório e não conta. Como todo caminho real de entrada (`OnboardingPage`/`WelcomePage`/`TripRecoveryScreen`) seta `onboardingCompleted: true`, usuários reais seguem reportando; drive-bys somem. **Fix (worker):** novo `DELETE /admin/installs` (purge-all, token-gated) → `TelemetryStore.deleteAllInstalls()` limpa `installs`+`heartbeats` (reset do lixo/teste; usuários reais re-reportam no próximo heartbeat). Worker redeployado `13e6bdd9`. Após o purge, store volta a 0 e só installs gated repovoam. **Nada removido (ÂNCORA 9).**
- **Last Green Test Run (0.99.36)**: **unit 1860/1860 (199 files)** (gate é um early-return de boundary impuro; coberto pelos testes de domínio de telemetria que já passam — sem nova lógica pura). `tsc --noEmit` app+worker limpo. `npm run build` green. **Worker smoke:** `DELETE /admin/installs` sem token→401, com token→`{ok,deleted:N}`; overview pós-purge→`total:0`.

- **🟢 0.99.35 (web/OTA + WORKER TelemetryStore) — Painel de Administração v1 · telemetria de uso anônima (nome SIM, valor NUNCA) + tela `/admin`.** Source: Julio — "um painel inicial de administração: quem está usando, com que frequência, quais funções, quantas compras dividiu/cadastrou, última vez que usou — sem dados sensíveis" + calibração: "quero NOME de quem usa e quanto usa, só não passar VALORES; consentimento on-by-default com opção de desativar; enviar ao abrir o app". **DEC-248.** **Arquitetura planner-na-nuvem/dados-no-servidor mínima (heartbeat, não event-stream):** (1) **Worker** ganhou o Durable Object **`TelemetryStore` (SQLite, migration `v5`)** + `POST /t` (ingest) + `GET /admin/*` (overview/installs/timeseries, **bearer `ADMIN_TOKEN`**) + `DELETE /admin/install` (limpar lixo/teste). Tabelas `installs` (1 linha/device: nome+contadores+flags+meta+`active_days`) e `heartbeats` (`install_id`+`day` → DAU/WAU/MAU). **Guarda de privacidade no servidor:** ingest só aceita chaves do allowlist (`TELEMETRY_COUNTERS`/`FLAGS`) — qualquer chave fora (ex.: `totalSpent`) → **HTTP 400**, então um valor monetário NUNCA é persistido. (2) **Cliente:** `domain/telemetry` PURO (`buildTelemetryPayload`/`deriveTelemetryFlags`/`shouldSendHeartbeat`/`utcDayKey`, clampa contadores, corta nome, dia UTC) + boundary `utils/telemetry.ts` (junta counts dos repos, nome do dono via `Participant.isOwner`, POSTa `/t` **≤1×/dia UTC**, best-effort/silencioso, respeita opt-out e pula demo) + `utils/telemetry-events.ts` (tally leve p/ `aiEntries`/`receiptScans`/`crashes` sem puxar o grafo de repos). Heartbeat disparado no `RootLayout` (app open + volta ao foreground). (3) **`AppSettings.telemetryEnabled` default true** (on-by-default) + toggle nas Configurações + aviso i18n pt/en/es ("dados anônimos p/ melhorar — nunca valores nem conteúdo dos gastos"). (4) **Painel `/admin`** (rota standalone, fora do shell, token-gated): KPIs (total de usuários desde sempre, DAU/WAU/MAU, novos 7d), barras de uso por funcionalidade, adoção (% por flag), distribuições (plataforma/versão/país) e **tabela de usuários com NOME + última vez + dias ativos + versão/plataforma/país + viagens/gastos/divisões/IA + remover**. Copy do painel em PT (ferramenta interna do Julio; código em inglês). **Nada removido (ÂNCORA 9).**

- **🟢 0.99.34 (web/OTA only) — Coerência do App · ÉPICO B2 (Wave 3) — "reconectar novo aparelho" + escolher amigo conectado no Acerto de contas.** Source: Julio — "Fazer o A": B2 Wave 3 (escopo novo) = "reconectar" ativo quando o `actorId` muda + escolher amigo conectado também na cobrança/dívida (até então só no dividir). **Decisão (conselho inline · Architect/Critic — conservador, anti-"dívida fantasma"):** a Wave 3 reusa 100% a camada de DADOS já existente (`peerLinks` global + `pairParticipantFromIdentity`) e adiciona só UX, com guardas duros. (1) **Picker de amigos no hub de cobrança:** `SharedExpensesPage` (Acerto de contas) ganhou, no form "adicionar pessoa", a lista "Amigos conectados" (`availableFriends` = peerLinks conectados que ainda NÃO são pessoa da viagem) → `handleAddFriend` adiciona o amigo como participante da viagem num toque via `pairParticipantFromIdentity` (mesma plumbing do split, sem digitar nome/QR). (2) **Reconectar aparelho novo:** núcleo PURO `domain/connections/connections.ts` → `findReconnectCandidate` só sugere quando (a) o participante TEM `linkedActorId`, (b) o link atual está **offline/ausente**, (c) existe **exatamente 1** candidato novo com `publicKey` + `displayName` de nome normalizado idêntico (`normalizeName`: sem acento/caixa) e `actorId` diferente — nunca adivinha em ambiguidade. O orquestrador `reconnectParticipantDevice` (sync-orchestrators) é **neutro no ledger**: só repõe `linkedActorId`, remapeia o `peerLink` novo p/ o participante e aposenta (soft-delete) o link antigo — **as fatias/dívidas do participante ficam intactas** (provado por teste). UI: botão "Reconectar novo aparelho de {nome}" por participante (não-dono) quando há candidato. i18n `connections.reconnect_device`/`connections.reconnected` pt/en/es. **Nada removido (ÂNCORA 9).** No schema/worker change.

- **🟢 0.99.31 (web/OTA only) — Coerência do App · ÉPICO C2 — ponte Saída→Divisão ("dividir esta saída"): mover, não duplicar.** Source: Julio (backlog estrutural — "Saída vs Mesa ao vivo: clarear quando usar cada um; possivelmente um ponto de entrada 'sozinho ou em grupo?'") + conselho `app-coherence-project §C2`. **Decisão do conselho (Chair, contra o ponto-de-entrada):** NÃO perguntar "sozinho ou em grupo?" na ABERTURA (a saída é um fluxo solo de primeira classe, vivido durante a noite; a divisão é o ato de FECHAR a noite). Em vez de bifurcar a entrada, expor a divisão como **ação disponível no fim da saída** — o Advocate ganhou (atrito zero pra quem só quer marcar bebida; a divisão aparece exatamente quando a conta chega). O Critic levantou o risco real: **gasto contado em dobro** (a saída já é um gasto; virar split não pode somar de novo). **Fix (aditivo, ZERO rewrite do split — reusa o mesmo motor da nota):** (1) builder PURO `domain/split/from-outing.ts` → `buildSplitFromOuting` espelha `buildSplitFromReceipt`: cada rodada (`amountCents>0`) vira `SplitItem` com descrição/categoria preservadas; dono é o único participante; sem persistência/transporte. (2) slot in-memory single-use `features/split/outing-split-handoff.ts` (espelha `receipt-split-handoff`): carrega a `SplitSession` pré-montada + `supersedeOutingSessionId` + ids das txs; nada sai do device. (3) `OutingPage` (SessionReview) ganhou botão secundário **"Dividir esta saída com outras pessoas"** (só com rodadas) → monta o split da saída, seta o handoff e navega `/split/scan`. (4) `SplitPage` consome o handoff no mount (efeito único, espera `trip`), abre direto em `divide` e guarda o `supersedeOutingRef`. (5) **`commitSplit`/`undoSplitCommit` ganharam `supersededOutingSessionId`** — no MESMO `db.transaction`, `softDeleteOutingRows` aposenta a Session+txs+sessionItems da saída de origem (move, não duplica); o `undoSplitCommit` faz `restoreOutingRows` (volta exatamente como estava, revision++). `resumedRef`/`resuming` semeados também por `hasPendingOutingSplitHandoff()` → o handoff vence o resume da mesa anterior, sem flash de "Retomando…". **Nada removido (ÂNCORA 9).** No schema/worker change.

- **🟢 0.99.29 (web/OTA + WORKER `58ff0a0e`) — IA Quick Entry · MULTI-GASTO: uma frase, vários gastos (frase real do Julio que falhava).** Source: Julio 2026-06-21 — "essa frase não está funcionando na IA: 'Saí com o Bruno e a Débora, o Bruno me pagou um sorvete de 2, a Débora uma água de 1, dividi um bolo de 10 com ela, paguei 4 de estacionamento e comprei uma pizza de 10 que dividimos nós 3' — deveria criar uma LISTA de gastos com junções, quem pagou quanto e quem deve a quem; testar com muitas frases complexas até funcionar". **Root cause (comprovado):** o roteador da nuvem (DEC-246) só devolvia UM `AiIntent` e o cliente só parseava um → numa frase de 5 eventos só o último sobrevivia. **Fix (arquitetura multi-action, planner-na-nuvem / executor-no-device preservada):** (1) **Worker `/assistant`** agora retorna `{"actions":[ ... ]}` — UM `AiIntent` por evento de dinheiro, na ordem; prompt reescrito com a regra "quantas ações" (split de N pessoas = 1 ação, NÃO N), exemplo multi-evento de 5 ações e `max_tokens` 500→2000 (uma história não trunca o JSON). (2) **`domain/assistant/intent.ts`** — `parseAssistantIntents` (lista) + `extractCandidates` aceita `{actions}`/`{intents}`/`{intent}`/array nu/objeto nu/string JSON; `parseAssistantResponse` virou wrapper do 1º evento (back-compat). (3) **novo núcleo PURO `domain/assistant/batch.ts` → `planAssistantBatch`** reusa o MESMO `buildActionPlan` por evento (sem matemática paralela): agrega TODAS as pessoas desconhecidas/ambíguas de todos os eventos numa ÚNICA clarificação (`add_people` — adiciona todo mundo num toque), e separa em `ready` (ops a confirmar) vs `blocked` (sem valor / moeda estrangeira que precisa de câmbio / navegar / não suportado — nada sumido em silêncio). (4) **`useAssistant`** ganhou fase `batch_preview`, `runBatch`/`confirmAddPeople`/`confirmBatch` (commita cada op pelo mesmo `executeOp`, junta os undos num "Desfazer" único, toast combinado). (5) **`AssistantSheet`** ganhou `BatchPreviewArea` (lista de eventos + "minha parte" total + 1 confirm) e o ramo `add_people` no `ClarifyArea`. i18n `batch.*`/`clarify.add_people`/`done.batch` pt-BR/en/es. **Nada removido (ÂNCORA 9)** — fluxo single-evento intocado; multi só dispara quando há >1 evento.

O único furo real era a ponte para a mesa **ao vivo**: uma nota lida em "Escanear nota" não virava pass-the-phone sem **re-escanear** pela outra porta. **Fix (cirúrgico, aditivo — zero rewrite do split, decisão alinhada ao Critic + mandato "sem regressão"):** (1) novo slot in-memory single-use `features/split/receipt-split-handoff.ts` (espelha `assistant-quickadd-draft.ts`: `set`/`take`/`hasPending`; carrega `ReceiptPlan` + a foto comprimida; nada sai do device). - **🟢 0.99.26 (web/OTA only) — Coerência do App · ONDA 7 — FEAT F9: mesa ao vivo reconhece o usuário do app + registra a parte dele no próprio app.** Source: Julio 2026-06-20 — "quando compartilho uma mesa ao vivo com alguém que já tem o app, ele ainda pergunta o nome e faz o fluxo como se a pessoa não tivesse o app, e no fim ele não pega o valor com os itens que ela selecionou e pergunta se quer criar um gasto no app dela com o que selecionou, falando quanto deu e como ficou no orçamento dela". **Achado-chave (comprovado no código):** o `/t/:id` (mesa do convidado) vive FORA do BootGate mas DENTRO do `AppDataProvider` (RootLayout monta o provider acima de TODAS as rotas) → `useAppData()` funciona ali e carrega a viagem do PRÓPRIO device. Logo `trip !== null` é o sinal exato de "este convidado também é usuário do app"; `trip === null` é um estranho de verdade. **Decisão de arquitetura (conselho inline · Architect/Critic/Advocate):** NÃO transformar a fatia do convidado num débito do dono automaticamente (o dono é a fonte da verdade DEC-106; mexer nisso é risco MÉDIO-ALTO) — em vez disso, registrar a fatia como o gasto PESSOAL do próprio convidado no app dele, reusando os MESMOS orquestradores do quick-add, idempotente. **Implementado:** (1) **núcleo PURO `domain/split/guest-self-expense.ts` → `planGuestSelfExpense(...)`** decide o que oferecer pela fatia: `none` (sem fatia/viagem quebrada) · `no_trip` (estranho → porta de onboarding T9) · `already` (idempotente — já registrado) · `currency_mismatch` (moeda da mesa ≠ base da viagem → entrega pré-preenchido ao editor completo, onde mora a UI de câmbio) · `ready` (mesmo-moeda → registra direto, fase+pote resolvidos). Sem ids/FX/efeitos. (2) **F9a — reconhecer o usuário:** `SplitTablePage` lê `participants`/`trip`; se há um perfil dono local e o convidado ainda não se nomeou, **adota o nome do próprio perfil e PULA o prompt de nome** (não trata mais como estranho); a porta de onboarding T9 só aparece para `trip === null`. (3) **F9b — registrar a própria parte:** quando a fatia é positiva e há viagem, CTA "Registrar minha parte no meu app" cria UMA tx pessoal (`createExpenseTransaction`→`registerExpense`, `walletId:null`, `externalRef: splitguest:<shareId>`, `excludeFromLearning`) e mostra o **impacto no orçamento** pela MESMA `useSplitBudgetReading` do dono (copy `split.reading_*`); idempotência por `getGuestCommit/setGuestCommit` (mapa shareId→txId em localStorage) + o `externalRef` → reabrir o link nunca duplica (estado `already`). Moeda diferente → `setAssistantQuickAddDraft`+`/quick-add` (mesma escotilha do AI quick-entry). **Nada removido (ÂNCORA 9).** No schema/worker change (mesa ao vivo já trafega tudo que precisa; o registro é 100% local no device do convidado).

(3) ambos commitam por `onAddNamedItem(cents, desc)` → `handleAddNamedItem` no pai (phase-gate + enrich, igual ao quick-add, só que com descrição real em vez do nome da saída). Lifecycle do mic limpo no unmount; degrada limpo (sem suporte/mic negado/offline/rate-limit → toast, a saída segue no toque). **Nada removido (ÂNCORA 9).** No schema/worker change (reusa `/ocr` e `/transcribe` já no ar).

- **🟢 0.99.24 (web/OTA only) — Coerência do App · ONDA 5 — FEAT F5b: reivindicar por UNIDADE em item com qty>1 (na divisão do dono).** Source: Julio 2026-06-20 — "se um item na nota tiver 2 pedidos, tem que ter isso no app, já que então 2 pessoas vão poder pegar esse item sem dividir, ela pagando o preço cheio do item". **Contexto (cadeia de qty já estava pronta):** o OCR endurecido (Onda 4) já entrega `qty` por linha → `parse.ts` carrega `qty`/`unitPrice` → `buildSplitFromReceipt` passa `qty` → `createSplitItem` deriva `unitAmountCents = amount/qty` → `claimWeight` já usa `units/qty` quando `claim.units !== null`. **O único furo era de UI:** não havia como marcar UNIDADES — então um item de 2 unidades, pego por 1 pessoa, cobrava a linha INTEIRA (preço dobrado), e o "2 pessoas pegam 1 cada pelo preço cheio" só dava via meia-divisão por fração. **Fix (cirúrgico, domínio + UI do dono):** (1) domínio puro `setClaimUnits(session, itemId, participantId, units)` em `split.ts` — fixa quantas unidades inteiras a pessoa pega (clamp `[0, qty]`; 0 = solta via `releaseClaim`; >0 = `claimItem({units})`), preservando as claims dos outros; + `claimedUnits(item, participantId)` (lê `units`, ou converte uma claim por fração de volta pra unidade `round(fraction*qty)`); ambos exportados do barrel. (2) `SplitPage` (visão "item" da divisão): item com `qty>1` ganha **seletor "− N +" por pessoa ativa** (cap em `qty`), subtítulo mostra `preço/un` + `N sem dono`/`todos pegos` e um selo `2×`; o TAP no item agora pega/solta **1 unidade** (gesto "tomei um desses") em vez da linha inteira — `qty===1` segue idêntico (toggle de fração ½/½, N-avos, sem regressão). (3) `ItemEditor` ganhou **controle de quantidade** (recalcula `unitAmountCents` ao mudar qty OU valor) pra corrigir no app quando a leitura errar o número de unidades. **Conflito real continua honesto:** 3 donos numa linha de 2 unidades → `detectClaimConflicts` sinaliza over-claim (o dono arbitra), igual ao caso qty===1. _(Mesa ao vivo do convidado `SplitTablePage` segue pegando linha inteira por toque — per-unit no lado convidado entra junto da F9; o protocolo já carrega `units`.)_ **Nada removido (ÂNCORA 9).** No schema/worker change.

- **🟢 0.99.22 (web/OTA only) — Coerência do App · ONDA 2 — FIX F2: gasto de "Dividir conta" sempre abre na divisão completa (e não duplica em Saídas).** Source: Julio 2026-06-20 — "quando crio um gasto a partir do dividir conta… no filtro Todos aparece como evento único e ao clicar mostra os itens como grupo restaurante, sem detalhar a divisão; só no filtro 'gastos sem carteira informada' aparece como gasto específico com quem dividi, quanto cada um pagou e a divisão completa — isso deveria aparecer sempre, a primeira tela está escondendo a melhor parte". **Root cause (comprovado no código):** `commitSplit` persiste a divisão como UMA `Session` "completed" embrulhando UMA transação do valor cheio (`externalRef: 'split:<id>'`) + o `SplitRecord` legível. No feed (`buildSessionFeed`), em modo BROWSING (sem filtro) toda tx com `sessionId` colapsa num rollup de sessão → a linha abria `/outings/:id/review` (lista de itens, **sem divisão**). O filtro "sem carteira" desliga o browsing (`isBrowsing=false`), então a MESMA tx aparecia solta e abria `/expenses/:id` (`ExpenseDetailPage`, que carrega o `SplitRecord` por `sessionId` e mostra a divisão completa) — por isso só o filtro mostrava "a melhor parte". Pior: a `Session` da divisão é `completed`, então ela ainda aparecia DE NOVO na aba **Saídas**. **Fix (cirúrgico, modelo coerente "divisão = gasto, mora só em Gastos"):** (1) predicado puro `isSplitCommitTransaction(tx)` + `SPLIT_REF_PREFIX` exportados de `domain/split/commit.ts` (orquestrador agora reusa o const em vez de duplicar a string); (2) `buildSessionFeed` **nunca** colapsa uma tx de split-commit — ela vira linha de gasto própria em qualquer modo, abrindo `/expenses/:id` → divisão completa sempre; (3) `ExpenseListPage` remove as sessões de split do `completedSessions` (aba Saídas) usando o set de `sessionId` das txs de split — some a duplicata. **Nada removido (ÂNCORA 9).** No schema/worker change.

- **🟢 0.99.19 (SHIPPED — web/OTA only) — AI Quick Entry · VOICE RELIABILITY FIX: singleton AudioContext (no start-loss, no repeat-failure) + getUserMedia fallback (DEC-246 follow-up to 0.99.18).** Source: Julio device-test 2026-06-20 — "ainda não está funcionando bem… funcionou 1 hora… ele não capturou o começo mas captou o fim do que eu falei… depois ficou dando 'não captei o áudio, tente de novo'… ele está flutuando, tem algum erro por trás. Faça testes internos, mande os próprios arquivos de áudio pela função, todos os tipos (3 s, 10 s)." **Root cause (proven by fake-mic Playwright probe, not guessed):** 0.99.18 captured a clean WAV but **created + `close()`d a fresh `AudioContext` per recording**. On the Android System WebView the 2nd/3rd context was born `suspended`, and a `resume()` fired outside a live user-gesture window silently failed → the node graph never delivered frames → the clip came back below the silence floor → "não captei o áudio" on every retry after the first (**repeat-failure**). Worse, even the working capture `resume()`d AFTER `getUserMedia` resolved, so the first ~300–500 ms of speech was dropped → "captou só o fim da frase" (**start-loss**). **Fix (`src/utils/audio-recorder.ts`, pure):** (1) **one shared `AudioContext` reused for the app lifetime** (`sharedContext`, recreated only if `closed`) — never per-recording close; `stop()` now only clears `onaudioprocess`, disconnects the nodes and stops the mic track (drops the recording indicator) while keeping the context **warm**, so capture #2..N are as healthy as #1. (2) **`resume()` BEFORE `getUserMedia`** (warm the graph first) so audio flows from the first frame — kills start-loss. (3) **graceful `getUserMedia` fallback** (`acquireMicStream`): try mono+echo/noise constraints, and on `NotSupportedError`/`OverconstrainedError`/`TypeError` fall back to plain `audio:true` instead of dead-ending the whole capture (some engines/devices reject the constrained request). The 0.99.18 WAV encoder, 16 kHz downsample, silence guard, `MIN_SPEECH_WAV_BYTES` accidental-tap guard and the `isLikelyVoiceHallucination` "E aí" filter are all unchanged underneath. **Nothing removed (ÂNCORA 9)** — narrowing/hardening of the existing primary voice path; MediaRecorder/webm fallback kept for engines without ScriptProcessor; no schema/worker change; no new APK (RECORD_AUDIO already in the 0.99.17 shell). Decision **DEC-246 (voice, follow-up)**.

The 2 `split-live-loop` integration failures are the pre-existing real-worker network tests (ETIMEDOUT to Cloudflare from this sandbox) — untouched. **Device-capture proof (fake-mic Playwright under xvfb, real pt-BR speech WAV):** headed Chromium with `--use-fake-device-for-media-stream` ran the REAL `startPcmRecording` 4× back-to-back → captured 3×7 s + 1×12 s WAVs; `ffmpeg silencedetect` confirmed **speech starts at t≈0 on every clip (no start-loss) and all four are full-length (no repeat-failure)**. **Endpoint proof:** pt-BR TTS (short + long) POSTed to the prod `/transcribe` returned the exact sentences — Whisper + the WAV format are correct for any duration, so the device bug was 100% client-side capture (now fixed).

- **🟢 0.99.18 (SHIPPED + VERIFIED LIVE — web/OTA only) — AI Quick Entry · VOICE FIX: real 16 kHz WAV capture + "E aí" no-speech guard (DEC-246 follow-up).** Source: Julio device-test 2026-06-20 — "a frase por texto funcionou, mas pelo microfone só aparece um 'E aí' na caixa e vem 'não entendi o que fazer'… a transcrição que está quebrada… pode usar IA para transcrever; pesquisar, implementar e fazer os deploys". **Root cause (proven, not guessed):** on the Android System WebView `MediaRecorder` emits Opus-in-webm **without duration cues** (a live stream), so Groq's server-side decoder read only the first fragment and Whisper returned a stray filler. **Live probe of the prod `/transcribe` route nailed it:** BOTH digital near-silence AND a pure 220 Hz tone return **exactly `{"text":" E aí"}`** — i.e. " E aí" is Whisper's universal pt-BR *"no intelligible speech"* output, not a transcription. So the captured audio reached Whisper but carried no real speech. **Fix (two parts, web/OTA):** (1) **new pure `src/utils/audio-recorder.ts`** — captures raw PCM via Web Audio (`getUserMedia`+`ScriptProcessor`, which also triggers the real RECORD_AUDIO prompt), downsamples to **16 kHz mono** (averaging decimator) and encodes a **self-contained 16-bit PCM WAV** (complete RIFF header + exact data length — the property a streaming webm lacks); a peak-amplitude silence guard returns an empty blob so a dead-mic capture never pays Whisper to hallucinate. `useAssistant.startWhisperCapture` now uses this as the PRIMARY voice path on native (MediaRecorder/webm kept only as a fallback for engines without ScriptProcessor); a `MIN_SPEECH_WAV_BYTES` guard short-circuits accidental taps to a `voice_unclear` toast. (2) **`isLikelyVoiceHallucination()` in `utils/ai-transcribe.ts`** — a transcript that is ONLY a known no-speech filler (e aí / obrigado / tchau / valeu / Amara.org…, accent- & punctuation-insensitive, **full-string match only** so "e aí, paguei 5 euros" passes through) maps to the same clear "não captei o áudio, tente de novo" toast instead of feeding "E aí" to the intent parser. **Nothing removed (ÂNCORA 9)** — additive over the existing voice path; the manifest RECORD_AUDIO permission already shipped in the 0.99.17 APK, so this OTA reaches installed apps with no new APK. Decision **DEC-246 (voice)**.

- **🟢 0.99.12 (SHIPPED — web/OTA + Worker redeploy) — AI Quick Entry: natural-language router (text + voice) wired to every core action (DEC-246).** Source: Julio — bar pain "toda vez que pego a bebida ir lá e marcar… parece que perco tempo marcando o que foi, onde foi"; wants one fast box (text **and** voice) — "o Bruno me pagou uma cerveja de 2 euros" — that infers place/time/person, asks only when needed (add/disambiguate), and routes to the right existing function; "expor todas as funções para a IA"; then "implementar tudo de uma vez sem parar". Plan + 5 inline councils: `brain/documents/ai-quick-entry-natural-language-router-plan-2026-06-20.md`. **Architecture = planner (cloud) / executor (device)** (the point Julio stressed: "não é chat bonitinho, é um interpretador de ações; o chat é a casca, o poder está no app expondo ferramentas seguras"). The cloud model (Groq `llama-3.3-70b-versatile`, **JSON mode**) is a pure **router**: it returns ONE typed `AiIntent` = an action + entities **by name/label**, never ids, never math. The **device** owns everything that matters. **New pure domain `src/domain/assistant/`:** `intent.ts` (Zod-coerced, untrusted-input-safe `parseAssistantResponse` + `isExecuteAction`), `context.ts` (`buildAssistantContext` — names-only pack), `resolve.ts` (`resolveCategory`/`resolveAmount`/`resolvePerson`/`resolveWallet`/`resolveDate`, accent-stripping + synonyms + 0/1/2+ person handling), `plan.ts` (`buildActionPlan` → ready `ExecOp` | `navigate` | `needs` clarification | `unsupported`, with `personOverrides` for re-plan-after-clarify), `dispatch.ts` (the ONLY impure boundary — `executeOp` runs through the SAME orchestrators QuickAdd uses: `resolvePayerExpense`→`registerExpense`, `registerIncome`, `transferBetweenWallets`, `withdrawCash`, `createSettlement`, `createPlannedPurchase`; each result carries an **undo**; `createAssistantParticipant` for "Adicionar Bruno?"), + barrel `index.ts`. **Mapped actions:** execute = `log_expense`·`someone_paid` (I owe)·`i_paid_for` (they owe me)·`split_expense`·`record_income`·`transfer`·`withdraw`·`settle_debt`·`plan_purchase`; navigate = `open_split_bill`·`open_scan_receipt`·`open_outing`·`open_plan_expense`·`open_simulator`·`open_screen` (debts/expenses/dashboard/wallets/planner/income/trip). **Worker (`worker/src/index.ts`):** stateless **`/assistant`** (chat→JSON, system-prompt built from the context pack) + **`/transcribe`** (Whisper `whisper-large-v3-turbo`) proxies added next to `/ocr` — the Groq key never leaves the Worker. **Client boundary:** `utils/ai-assistant.ts` + `utils/ai-transcribe.ts`. **UI (`src/features/assistant/`):** `assistant-bus.ts` (open-from-anywhere event bus), `useAssistant.ts` (state machine: input→thinking/transcribing→preview→clarify→saving→done/error; Web Speech first, Whisper fallback), `AssistantSheet.tsx` (`BottomSheet`: text box + mic, one-tap **preview→confirm**, clarify tap-loop, undo toast, manual fallback). **Wiring:** a gradient **AI hero** at the base of the `FAB` (the new #1, gated by `aiQuickEntryEnabled`), `AssistantSheet` mounted globally in `AppShell`. **Privacy (ÂNCORA 8):** `AppSettings.aiQuickEntryEnabled` (default **ON** — owner's headline tool, degrades gracefully when key/network absent) + `aiQuickEntryPrivateNames` (opt-in; withholds people/wallet names → always ask "who?"); seed + repo backfill (ON / private-off); Settings toggles added. Context pack = first names/labels + place label + language + base currency + category list; **never** amounts/balances/ids/history. **Correctness:** "o Bruno me pagou uma cerveja" → `someone_paid` → `resolvePayerExpense` row 4 (payer=Bruno, not split) → I owe the full amount, no wallet move; a non-connected Bruno is born **confirmed** (DEC-241). i18n `assistant.*` namespace added pt-BR/en/es. **Nothing removed (ÂNCORA 9)** — additive over existing orchestrators; the existing `parseVoiceExpense` regex path stays as the offline fallback; no schema change; the Worker proxies are additive. Decision **DEC-246**.

- **🟢 0.99.8 (deployed, web-only OTA) — UI regression batch: scrollbars hidden again app-wide (D-BUG-21), calendar/day amounts carry the currency symbol (D-BUG-22), expense multi-select bar shows EVERY action (D-BUG-23), + a latent Node-20 WebCrypto fix (D-BUG-24).** Source: Julio (device, post-G3) — "todas as telas voltaram a ter scrollbar… corrigir, prova com Playwright; nos gastos do dia e no calendário o valor está sem o símbolo da moeda; ao selecionar gastos a barrinha esconde as outras opções atrás — achar uma forma melhor de mostrar". **D-BUG-21 — scrollbars (`styles/globals.css`):** the app-wide hide was set on `html` only, but Firefox honors `scrollbar-width` **per scroll container**, so every nested overflow area (bottom sheets, lists, settings panes) re-exposed a bar — the "scrollbars came back on every screen" regression. Hardened to `html, body, *` for `scrollbar-width:none` (+ legacy `-ms-overflow-style`) and `*::-webkit-scrollbar { width:0!important; height:0!important; display:none!important }` (the `!important` defeats any later utility/inline override); the zoomed native root rule (D-BUG-07) kept. Content stays fully scrollable; only the visual bar is gone. **D-BUG-22 — currency symbol (`domain/money/money.ts` + the two calendar cards):** `formatMoneyCompact` was deliberately symbol-less, so "Gastos do dia" + the "Gastos/Disponível por dia" calendars rendered a bare number ("46"). Added an optional `currency` param that prepends the **narrowSymbol** ("€46", "R$1,2k", sign stays leftmost "-€46") via `Intl.NumberFormat...formatToParts`; `HeatmapGrid` + `AvailableCalendar` now pass `currency` (legacy callers that omit it stay byte-identical). **D-BUG-23 — multi-select bar (`components/SelectionBar.tsx` + `ExpenseListPage.tsx`):** the old single-row `justify-end overflow-x-auto` strip pushed the leftmost actions off-screen BEHIND the "N selecionados" label with the scrollbar hidden → invisible options. Rebuilt as **two tiers** — count + cancel on top, then a row of **equal-width `flex-1` buttons with the icon stacked over the label** (no truncation of "Mover de fundo"), always fully on-screen for any action count; list bottom padding `pb-24`→`pb-32`; stable `data-selection-bar`/`data-expense-row` hooks for E2E. **D-BUG-24 — WebCrypto (`data/sync/crypto.ts`):** the full suite under the project-pinned **Node 20** surfaced a latent `importKey`/`decrypt` `ArrayBuffer` type error (`bytes.buffer` could be a pooled/`SharedArrayBuffer`-typed buffer, rejected by Node-20's stricter WebCrypto). Switched to `.slice()` (a fresh exact `Uint8Array<ArrayBuffer>` — valid `BufferSource`, identical in the browser). **Nothing removed (ÂNCORA 9)** — all additive/narrowing; no schema/worker change; web/OTA only; APK preserved byte-identical at the verified 0.56.0 shell.

- **🛠️ 0.99.7 (infra hotfix) — apex outage fixed + Cloudflare Pages Git auto-build repaired.** Source: Julio — "o commit 57bf846 já está em produção mas o website não está acessível". **Root cause:** the Pages project has the **GitHub integration connected** (`deployments_enabled` → auto-deploy on every push to `master`) but with an **empty `build_config`** (`root_dir`/`build_command`/`destination_dir` all blank). So the `git push` of v0.99.7 triggered an auto-build that served the **repo root** — which has no `index.html` (the app lives in `TripPilot/`) — producing an empty deployment (`e8257c2a`) that took over the apex → **404 on everything** (incl. `/version.json`). The correct v0.99.7 content was still live on the direct-upload deployment (`0a97c841`); only the apex pointer was hijacked by the broken CI build. **Fix (two parts):** (1) **restored the apex immediately** with a direct `wrangler pages deploy dist` (`a98e00da`, source 57bf846) — apex back to 200 + 0.99.7. (2) **made the auto-build correct** so a push now publishes the SAME artifact set as the manual flow: set `build_config` via the CF API to **`root_dir=TripPilot`, `destination_dir=dist`, `build_command="npm run build:pages"`** (+`build_caching`); new **`build:pages`** script = `npm run build` → `scripts/fetch-live-apk.mjs` (re-fetch the byte-identical live `/trippilot.apk`, **non-fatal** on miss — the web app + OTA bundle don't contain the APK) → `scripts/make-ota-bundle.mjs` (web zip `bundles/<v>.zip`); `.node-version` pins Node 20 for the build image. **Verified end-to-end:** the push of `b9b44f3` auto-built `b32a1eef` (stage deploy = **success**) and the apex now serves `/`+`/dashboard`+`/shared` = 200, `version.json` 0.99.7, `bundles/0.99.7.zip` (783,463 B), `/trippilot.apk` byte-identical (8,283,527 B), `sw.js` `trippilot-v55`, entry JS `index-DxA9BjgE.js` = 0.99.7. **A `git push` to `master` now deploys correctly on its own** (direct `wrangler` upload remains available as the manual/override path). Commit `b9b44f3`.

Full plan: `brain/documents/debt-and-split-ux-deep-dive-and-plan-2026-06-19.md`. **Root cause (deep-dive):** owner-authored third-party shares were born `confirmationStatus:'pending'`, and `calculateDebts` counts ONLY `confirmed` shares → an offline friend's debt was **invisible on the owner's ledger** until someone confirmed it through a synced channel an app-less friend never has (the contradictory UX: an "Em dia" balance next to a "Pendente" slice, the owner nagged to confirm entries they themselves authored). **Fix (pure domain, DL-1):** new `resolveShareBirthStatus(participantId, isPayerOrOwner, connectedSet)` in `domain/splitting/splitting.ts` — the owner/payer **and every NON-connected third party are born `confirmed`** (the debt is real the instant it's registered, DEC-106: owner = source of truth); only a **CONNECTED** counterparty (paired device `linkedActorId` / live `actorId`) stays `pending`, so the DEC-071/106 mirror confirm/reject still flows. Threaded an optional `connectedParticipantIds` into **`resolvePayerExpense`** (QuickAdd + OutingPage enrich/split pass a `linkedActorId`-based set; receipt/import/round paths default to none → born confirmed) and **`commitSplit`** (derives its connected set from each `SplitParticipant.actorId`, so an ad-hoc person just promoted to a trip Participant owes immediately while a live app-guest stays pending). Demo's offline "Ana" share flipped pending→confirmed. **Surfaces with ZERO UI change:** `/shared`, the dashboard debts and Copiloto all read `calculateDebts`, so offline-friend debts now appear at once; `findPendingConfirmationShares` (the "pendências de confirmação" card) now lists ONLY connected-pending shares (the owner is no longer nagged to confirm an app-less friend's debt) — DL-2's "Pendente = connected awaiting acceptance" intent already realized at the data layer. The "Acerto de contas" hub redesign (DL-3/4) = **G2 (v0.99.6)**; remind/cobrar (DL-5) = **G3 (v0.99.7)**. **Nothing removed (ÂNCORA 9)** — `buildSharesWithPayer`/`createEqualShares` (the edit/enrich/crud paths) untouched; the change is additive at the two owner-authored creation chokepoints. - **🟢 0.99.2 (deployed, web-only OTA + worker already live) — Bill Split · live-table FIX VERIFIED ON TWO DEVICES + 3-option share sheet.** Source: Julio — "fiz de tudo com dois celulares e não atualiza um pro outro; a mesa ao vivo sempre fica esperando alguém entrar enquanto o outro já vê os itens… só pare quando conseguir testar e ver que realmente está funcionando". **The real blocker on real devices was the SERVICE WORKER, not the backend.** Diagnosis (browser GET count vs worker-received count): the old `public/sw.js` ran `cacheFirst` for EVERY GET, so it cached the first (empty) cross-origin `/share/:id/responses` poll and served it forever — the owner's polls never reached the worker (stuck on "esperando alguém entrar"), and the guest's never saw fresh data. `Cache-Control: no-store` + `cache:'no-store'` were already correct but the SW intercepts before them. **Fix (`public/sw.js`, CACHE_NAME→`trippilot-v50`):** the fetch handler now `return`s immediately for any cross-origin request (`url.origin !== self.location.origin`) and only ever cache-firsts same-origin static assets (`/assets/`, `/icons/`, shell) — every live-share poll now hits the network. This complements the 0.99.0/0.99.1 **DO migration** (`ShareStore`, `SHARE_STORE_DO`, migration v4) that gave read-after-write (KV's eventual consistency was the *secondary* staleness source; the worker was already redeployed + live). **Proven 4 ways (real tests, per the directive):** (1) production worker curl cycle — create→read→2 guests post→immediate read sees BOTH→revoke→410; (2) `e2e/split-live-2device.spec.ts` (NEW, two SEPARATE browser contexts) vs the dev server on the prod worker — owner leaves "esperando" and shows "1 marcando agora", guest sees an item the owner adds LATER (two-way), GETs leave the browser >1; (3) the SAME spec vs a `vite preview` of the production build with the **real Service Worker registered + controlling the page** (the exact failure scenario) — green; (4) the SAME spec vs the **LIVE production apex** `https://trippilot.pages.dev` — green. Plus `src/tests/integration/split-live-loop.test.ts` (real worker) proving read-after-write (claim visible +62ms after post). **3-option share sheet (`LiveShareSheet` in `SplitPage.tsx`):** tapping "Compartilhar link" on the live card now opens a `BottomSheet` with a **QR code** (`QrCodeDisplay`, for phones physically together), **Copiar link** (clipboard) and **Compartilhar link** (native share sheet via `shareOrCopyLink` — fixes "no app instalado só copiava"). i18n `splitTable.copy_link/share_sheet_title/share_qr_hint` (pt-BR/en/es). **Nothing removed (ÂNCORA 9)** — the SW change is a narrowing (it now caches strictly less); the share sheet is additive over the existing button.

- **🟢 0.99.1 (deployed, web-only OTA) — Paired share · EPIC B — two-device "split with a connected user" now AUTO-SENDS + peer foreground re-pull.** Source: Julio — "ja faz a divisão de gastos que também tem esse mesmo tipo de conexão funcionar da melhor forma… quando eu escolho dividir com um user que já está conectado com minha conta ele já manda para o user… melhorar essa parte completa também de divisão estando realmente entre dois aparelhos". **Exploration first (mapped, not rebuilt):** the two-device paired flow already exists and is hardened — **DEC-207 share-link** is the real-time channel (owner `ShareLinkSheet` create/refresh/pull + `'upd'`/`'resp'` `ShareSignal`; peer `MirroredStatementsSection` keeps one live socket per share-statement, confirm/reject pushes back via `answerAndPushShareLine` and nudges the owner) with the **DEC-106 mirror mailbox** as the offline fallback; all transport is E2E-encrypted on the same worker endpoints proven in 0.99.0's 2-client smoke. **Two real gaps vs "ele já manda":** (1) the owner had to MANUALLY tap "Atualizar" to push a fresh split to an already-connected peer; (2) the peer had NO foreground re-pull (unlike the live table we just hardened). **B1 — auto-push on open (`ShareLinkSheet.tsx`):** extracted `publishLatest(announce)` from `handleRefresh`, then added a once-per-mount effect — opening a participant's sheet that ALREADY has a link (e.g. arriving from the post-split "compartilhar com {name}" nudge, which navigates to `/shared` and opens exactly this sheet) silently re-publishes the latest statement (`refreshShareLink`, revision++) and sends `'upd'`, so the new split reaches the connected peer's device with zero manual taps. No link yet → nothing pushed (the owner still generates + shares the URL once, out-of-band — that first hop cannot be automated). The sheet remounts per participant, so the guard is correct. **B2 — peer foreground re-pull (`MirroredStatementsSection.tsx`):** a `visibilitychange`→visible listener silently re-pulls EVERY share-origin mirrored statement (`refreshSharedLink`) and updates an open sheet, so a peer that missed an `'upd'` while backgrounded (mobile throttles timers + drops the socket) sees the owner's latest the instant they return — the exact resilience pattern shipped for the live table in 0.98.1/0.99.0. **Nothing removed (ÂNCORA 9)** — both are additive UI wiring reusing existing orchestrators; no new abstraction, no schema/worker change, E2E intact (per-participant `/s/:id` AES, key in the URL fragment).

No new pure logic (UI wiring over already-tested orchestrators), so coverage is the existing suites + the live two-device e2e. - **🟢 0.99.0 (deployed, web-only OTA + worker) — Bill Split · Live-table RESILIENCE — server-truth survival (works with the organizer offline), real connection status, owner auto-resume, manual reconnect.** Source: Julio device reports — "não tem como fazer a mesa ao vivo funcionar mesmo se o organizador fechar por engano? ele funcionar no server por um tempo? e tem que ter algo que mostre o status real de conexão (os apps falavam 'sincronizado' e não estava)"; "Todos tem que ter uma fonte única da verdade e aparecer em tempo real no celular de todos o que cada um escolhe, mesmo que fique no servidor"; "quebrar a criptografia E2E não é um grande problema se precisar". **Inline council (Architect / Security / User-Advocate / Realtime) → deterministic client-side reduction, E2E KEPT** (dropping it proved unnecessary). This directly resolves the 0.98.1 "owner-reducer" caveat: the table no longer depends on the owner's device relaying. **L1 — server is the source of truth (`worker/src/index.ts`, deployed):** `GET /share/:id/responses` is now OPEN (no write token) — any link-holder already reads the statement, so it may also read the (still-encrypted) responses. Mutations (PUT/DELETE) stay write-token-gated. **L1 client (`live-link.ts` + `SplitTablePage.tsx`):** both owner AND guest now pull `statement + ALL responses`, decrypt them (`decodeResponses`, shared), and apply the SAME deterministic `reduceGuestClaims` locally → every device converges on an identical merged table with or without the organizer present. The guest gained `fetchSplitResponses` (id+key only) and reacts to ANY signal frame (`upd`/`resp`). Optimistic local echo retained (a tap shows instantly via local `mine`). **L2.M4 — honest status (`live-status.ts` + `LiveStatusBadge.tsx`):** a pure `liveConnState` derives live/syncing/offline from **real pull freshness** (≤9 s live, ≤20 s syncing, else offline) + the socket; the self-contained badge owns its own clock + i18n and is rendered identically by the owner card and the guest page — the static "Sincronizado" (which once lied) is gone. `connectShareSignal` gained an `onStatus` open/close callback. **L2.M5 — owner auto-resume (`useSplitLiveLink.ts` + `SplitPage.tsx`):** the owner's creds persist in `localStorage` (`split.owner.live`); on reopen `SplitPage` re-fetches the statement from the server (the truth) and `live.resume()` re-attaches the SAME link without minting a new one — it primes `lastPublishedRef` from the server session so the first reconcile is a no-op (never clobbers). A revoked/missing/corrupt table is forgotten silently; `stop()`/commit clears it. The worker auto-increments any PUT revision (`body.revision > record.revision ? … : record.revision+1`), so resume can never be rejected or roll state back. **L2.M6 — honest TTL + reconnect:** server TTL was already honest (statement + responses = **90-day sliding** refreshed on every write; revoke writes a **14-day tombstone** → reads 410), so "funciona no servidor por um tempo" needed no change; the guest already had a manual reconnect, and the owner gained one (`reconnect()` = force pull; the socket self-heals via the existing capped backoff). i18n `splitTable.resuming` (pt-BR/en/es). **Nothing removed (ÂNCORA 9)** — purely additive; the only auth relaxation is the responses READ (by design). KV schema untouched.

- **🟢 0.98.1 (deployed, web-only OTA) — Bill Split · FIX — live-table share link no longer "localhost" + foreground re-sync.** Source: Julio device report 2026-06-18 ("na hora de compartilhar via link a divisão ele esta mandando como localhost… mudei manual… mas não atualiza nos celulares as escolhas dos outros"). **Root cause (the dead link):** the G2 owner link builder `buildSplitTableLink` (`features/split/live-link.ts`) used raw `window.location.origin`, which inside the Capacitor WebView is `https://localhost` (`androidScheme:'https'`) — the exact **D-BUG-01** class already fixed for `/s/:id`+`/pair` in v0.71.0 via `getShareOrigin()`, but the NEW split-table link (G2) never adopted it. **Fix:** `buildSplitTableLink` now uses `getShareOrigin()` (canonical `https://trippilot.pages.dev` on native, real origin on web/PWA), so the Web-Share/clipboard link the owner sends carries the right address — the pure builder `buildSplitTableUrl` is unchanged (only the origin SOURCE). **Second symptom (no sync) diagnosed, not a worker bug:** the live worker round-trip was probed end-to-end and is healthy — `POST /share` (create), `GET /share/:id` (statement), `POST /share/:id/responses` (guest), `GET /share/:id/responses` (owner, 200 + 403 without token), `/share/:id/ws` (426 = exists), `DELETE` (revoke) all correct; ALL transport uses the FIXED worker URL (`trippilot-sync.workers.dev`, not origin), so the `localhost` LINK was the blocker (guests on a localhost URL never loaded → nothing to propagate). **Robustness add:** both the owner hook (`useSplitLiveLink`) and the guest page (`SplitTablePage`) now pull/refetch on `visibilitychange`→visible, so claims that arrived while the app was backgrounded (mobile throttles timers + drops the socket) appear the instant the owner/guest returns instead of waiting for the next 6 s poll. **Known caveat (owner-reducer, by design):** the owner's device is the live relay — guests see each other only while the owner's SplitPage stays open; navigating away ends the live table (re-tapping "Mesa ao vivo" mints a NEW link). **Nothing removed (ÂNCORA 9)** — pure additive fix. Backend/schema/worker untouched; web/OTA only; APK preserved byte-identical at the verified 0.56.0 shell.

- **🟢 0.97.0 (deployed, web-only OTA) — Bill Split EPIC · G2 — Live table link (owner publishes, guests claim on their own phone) COMPLETE.** Source: `brain/documents/bill-split-implementation-support-2026-06-18.md` (gates G1–G3) + `bill-split-feature-brainstorm-and-council-2026-06-18.md`; decisions T1–T17. Second gate of the bill-split epic — turns the single-device divide board (G1) into a **live table**: the owner shares a link, everyone claims their own items on their own phone, and the owner's device stays the single source of truth (two-way propagation hardening = G3). **Reuses the DEC-207 share channel end-to-end** (encrypted KV `/share`, `ShareSignal` WS relay, AES key in the URL fragment the worker never sees) — no new backend. **G2.M1 — pure domain (`domain/split/`):** `share-payload.ts` (`splitSharePayloadSchema` — a Zod-hardened wrapper that publishes the live `SplitSession` with a revision + timestamp) and `claim-response.ts` (`splitClaimResponseSchema` — a guest's self-healing claim **snapshot** — + the **owner-reducer `reduceGuestClaims`** that folds guest snapshots into the live session, **owner-authoritative**: a guest can only ever set its OWN slice, never the owner's; `markedPaid` slices are frozen; unknown items ignored; latest snapshot per actor wins). **G2.M2 — owner orchestration (`features/split/live-link.ts` + `useSplitLiveLink.ts`):** publish/republish/pull/revoke wrappers (crypto + share-client glue, all best-effort, never throw into the UI) + guest identity (stable per-device actorId); the hook owns the lifecycle — `start()` publishes, a signal channel + 6 s poll floor pull guest claims on every `resp` ping, and ANY session change (owner edit OR a merged claim) **debounce-republishes** with an `upd` ping, guarded by a value-compare so an idempotent re-pull never spams KV. **G2.M3 — guest live board (`features/split/SplitTablePage.tsx`, route `/t/:id#k=` OUTSIDE BootGate** so a guest with no trip is never bounced to onboarding): asks the guest's name once, seeds their prior claims from the published session, lets them tap their items (optimistic), shows **"sua parte" computed by the EXACT domain math** (`reduceGuestClaims` + `computeSplitTotals` — WYSIWYG) + the whole-bill total, posts a snapshot (debounced, idempotent by actorId) and re-pulls on `upd` pings. **G2.M4 — owner UI (`SplitPage.tsx` `LiveTableCard`):** "Mesa ao vivo" invite → share via Web Share (clipboard fallback) → live panel (guest count + end), guest claims merge in automatically; the link is **revoked on commit**. New `buildSplitTableUrl` (`/t/` prefix, distinct from `/s/`). i18n `splitTable.*` pt-BR/en/es. **Nothing removed (ÂNCORA 9)** — G1 single-device flow untouched (the live table is purely additive); the persistent-statement `/s/:id` mirror is independent. Backend/schema untouched; web/OTA only; APK preserved byte-identical at the verified 0.56.0 shell.

He asked the **council** to re-rank the actions. **Inline council (User Advocate / Architect / Critic / Strategist) → promote planning, demote mercado.** The visible secondary tier must hold DISTINCT, high-intent actions: "Iniciar saída" (a live-capture session) and the two PLANNING tools (the app's differentiators vs. a plain tracker). The "Planejar" expander was removed entirely (its `OTHER_EXPANDER` is the only collapsible group now; `openGroups` Record collapsed to a single `otherOpen` boolean). Render order top→bottom (bottom = thumb): "Outros registros" (collapsed) → planning chips → "Iniciar saída" → scan hero → register hero. Plan chips use `grid-cols-1` when only one is visible (simple mode, where Simular is advanced-hidden) so there's never a lopsided empty cell. **Nothing removed (ÂNCORA 9)** — all 9 actions present and reachable; simple mode still hides advanced (`visibleInMode`). i18n: `fab.group_other_desc` now names mercado ("Mercado, transferência, saque, entrada" / "Groceries, transfer, withdrawal, income" / "Mercado, transferencia, retiro, ingreso"); the now-unused `fab.group_plan`/`fab.group_plan_desc` keys were removed from all three locales. - **🟢 0.93.0 (deployed, web-only OTA) — Device Test 2026-06-18 · GATE 17 — Home occasion carousel opens on the planned metas COMPLETE.** Source: Julio device test 2026-06-18 — "os cards em carrossel na página de início… ele está entrando já e o primeiro card que ele está mostrando é o outros gastos (42)… se eu quiser ver o planejamento tenho que rolar". He asked the **council** to decide whether planned-first or spend-desc is right. **Inline council (User Advocate / Architect / Critic / Strategist) → planned-first stays:** the metas (bar/restaurante/mercado) are the user's stated intention, carry the app's colour+icon language, and the count cards ("Outros 42") are raw activity, not goals — the leftmost card is the highest-value glance, so it must be a meta. **The ordering was already planned-first** (`domain/dashboard/occasion-counters.ts` → `buildOccasionCounters`, planned metas before activity counts since v0.31.0; verified via git + a Playwright probe of Julio's backup: the DOM order is correct). **The real bug was the scroll POSITION, not the order.** Verified cause (Playwright, Julio's backup, NO JS scroll involved): the carousel was a `scroll-snap-type: x` container, and **when it scrolls INTO the viewport** (the user swiping the page down) the browser **re-snaps it to card 3** (scrollLeft 373 of 497 = the first "Outros" activity card) — so it *looked* like it opened on Outros. Isolation matrix: `snap-mandatory` → jumps to 373; `snap-proximity` → still 373; **`snap-none` → holds at 0** even after scroll-into-view (FINAL 0/0). **Fix (`features/dashboard/DashboardCards.tsx`):** the occasion carousel drops scroll-snap (`snap-x snap-mandatory` → `snap-none`) so the browser never re-snaps it off the first meta; it scrolls freely and the existing dot pager still tracks position via `onScroll`. The now-inert `snap-start snap-always` on the card wrappers was removed. A cheap mount-time `scrollLeft = 0` pin (ref callback, +1 rAF) guards against any device scroll-restoration on back-nav. **Trade-off (documented):** forced 3-per-page snapping on THIS one carousel is gone (free scroll instead) — the only reliable way to stop Chromium's involuntary re-snap; the dots remain. Decision **DEC-237**. Backend/schema untouched; web/OTA only; APK preserved byte-identical at the verified 0.56.0 shell.

The snap fix is documented in code + DEC-237 (a component test can't exercise scroll-snap in jsdom, and the demo data seeds no occasion carousel, so the unit test locks ordering and the code comment + DEC lock the no-snap rationale).

- **🟢 0.92.0 (deployed, web-only OTA) — Device Test 2026-06-18 · GATE 16 — Amigo Sincero deep redesign (phase-truth dominance, correct culprit, present-tense reserve copy, recovery-mode rescue) COMPLETE.** Source: Julio device test 2026-06-18 — "o amigo sincero a meio complicado quando falta dinheiro", reproduced from his backup (`trippilot-backup-2026-06-18-0928.json`): a €600 "Outros" drained the Burgos phase, yet the card said "cabem só 1 dos 2 dentro do plano" (a lie), blamed a €3 bar, projected a reserve date that was already today, and the rescue offered a "guardar X" calculator that fit nothing. **Root cause (the lie):** the broke check read the **pool** free-to-spend, still **+€127.71** (the €150 protected reserve was untouched), while the **TRUE free** the user actually sees (hero "livre" = pool free − the scenario plan still reserved) was **−€22.54 (€0 clamped)** — so the card ran the category-plan path instead of telling the truth. **Fix (`domain/budget/honest-friend.ts` + `budget.ts`):** a new dominant **`over_budget`** kind pre-empts every category read when TRUE free ≤ 0, with case-precise copy — **reserve dip** (pool free < 0 → "já usou €X da reserva protegida"), **plan-committed** (reserve intact but plan needs more than what's left → "o que resta está reservado pro seu plano — faltam €X"), or **edge** (exactly 0); tone always `alert`. The signed pool free is exposed once at the source as `FreeToSpendResult.freeToSpendRawCents` (additive); TRUE free = `poolFreeRaw − planReserved`, computed identically in `useDashboardModel`, `ImpactDetailPage` and `RescuePage` so the three never disagree. **Non-profile blindness (`useDashboardModel.ts`):** the trigger spend is no longer filtered to activity-profile expenses (a plain "Outros" is finally seen); `amigoProfile` is now optional and `no_plan` dropped the profile label. **Culprit (`ImpactDetailPage.tsx`):** now the **biggest expense of the phase** (any category — "Maior gasto desta fase"), so it names the real cause (€600), not a €3 beer; a dynamic phase-broke banner (reserve/plan/edge) was added and the reserve-date copy goes present-tense ("A reserva protegida já está sendo usada agora") when the date is today/past. **Rescue → recovery (`RescuePage.tsx`):** when TRUE free ≤ 0 the page drops the infeasible "guardar X" calculator and flips to a recovery plan — a red truth banner (same three cases) + "O que dá pra cortar" listing the remaining planned occasions sorted by value with a "cortando tudo, você recupera €Y" total (still nothing persisted). i18n pt-BR/en/es (`amigo_over_budget(_reserve|_plan|_edge)`, `amigo_recover_cta`, `impact.biggest|phase_broke_*|reserve_in_use_now`, `rescue.broke_*|recover_*`). Decision **DEC-236**. Backend/schema untouched; web/OTA only; APK preserved byte-identical at the verified 0.56.0 shell.

The healthy reads (`on_plan`/`over_pace`/`over_plan`/`no_plan`) are gated behind `over_budget`, untouched when free > 0; `freeToSpendRawCents` added to the two `FreeToSpendResult` fixture literals (`fts-breakdown`, `true-free`). tsc clean; web build green (largest chunk vendor-react 289.90 KB < 500 KB). **Playwright visual QA (Pixel 5)**: imported Julio's **real backup** and confirmed all three screens — the Home card ("Acabou o dinheiro livre desta fase" + "Você já usou €72,29 da sua reserva protegida" + "Como me recuperar"), the Impact page ("Esta fase ficou sem dinheiro livre" banner + "MAIOR GASTO DESTA FASE: Outros €600,00"), and the Rescue page ("O QUE DÁ PRA CORTAR" recovery list) — every complaint resolved.

Backend/schema untouched; web/OTA only; APK preserved byte-identical at the verified 0.56.0 shell.

- **🟢 0.80.0 (deployed, web-only OTA) — Pacote #1 (Budget Model Reform) · GATE 5 — Progressive wallet tracking COMPLETE.** Plan: `brain/documents/budget-model-implementation-prompt-2026-06-17.md` (decisions in `budget-model-master-decision-2026-06-17.md`). D10/D11: the "de onde saiu o dinheiro?" wallet question is now INVISIBLE for a single-source traveler and lights up automatically once there are 2+ wallets OR a Wise import; a manual control in Settings overrides the automatic rule either way. **M5.1** pure `isWalletTrackingActive(wallets, override, hasWiseImport)` (`domain/wallets/wallets.ts`) — `override===null` → auto (`countActiveWallets>=2 || hasWiseImport`), `true` → always on, `false` → always off — plus pure helpers `hasWiseImportedTransactions` (any live tx whose `externalRef` starts with `wise:`) and `countActiveWallets`. New non-indexed setting `walletTrackingOverride: boolean|null` (default null = auto; additive, backfilled in the repo, passes through the backup schema). Settings → "Dispositivo e captura" gets a 3-state control (Automático · Sempre perguntar · Nunca perguntar) above the default-wallet picker with a live "Agora: ligado/desligado" hint. **M5.2/M5.3** a shared `useWalletTracking()` hook (reads the app-data snapshot) gates the wallet picker on **Quick Add** (primary), **expense edit**, **Income** and **Outing review** — when off the picker is hidden and the expense silently uses the default wallet (so a never-touched flow is byte-identical to before; the "Carteira não informada"/DEC-051 option only appears when tracking is on). Wise-imported trips stay coherent (they auto-activate tracking and remain wallet-bound). Backend/schema untouched (one additive non-indexed setting); web/OTA only; APK preserved byte-identical at the verified 0.56.0 shell.

- **🟢 0.79.0 (deployed, web-only OTA) — Pacote #1 (Budget Model Reform) · GATE 4 — First-class Events + single planning door (2 questions × 3 funding) COMPLETE.** Plan: `brain/documents/budget-model-implementation-prompt-2026-06-17.md` (decisions in `budget-model-master-decision-2026-06-17.md`). The Evento×Pote confusion is dissolved into ONE guided door: **"Planejar um gasto"** asks (1) *tem data?* and (2) *de onde vem o dinheiro?* (3 funding options: do dia a dia · um valor à parte só pra isso · um pote que já existe) and the app silently creates the right entity — Evento, Evento+Pote novo, Evento+Pote existente, Compra planejada, or Pote — never exposing the technical names. **M4.1** pure `routePlannedExpense({ hasDate, funding })` → `PlannedExpenseOutcome` (`domain/planning/plan-routing.ts`) is the whole 2×3 truth table; atomic orchestrator `createPlannedExpense` (`domain/orchestrators/plan-orchestrators.ts`) wires the entities in ONE Dexie transaction per outcome (event reserves from its owner trecho when phase-funded; pot-funded events leave the trecho's free-to-spend intact; pure-pot creation carries the optional `goalCents` preserved from GATE 3). **M4.2** new guided `PlanExpenseSheet` (`features/trip/PlanExpenseSheet.tsx`) replaces the standalone `CreatePotSheet` — two choice-card questions, example chips that prefill the name (master §10.4), an optional savings-goal toggle exposed ONLY for a pure Pote (no GATE-3 regression), and a "why" microcopy. **Single door** the FAB action and the TripHub CTA both open it via `/viagem?plan=1`. **M4.3** funding 3 ways verified by unit tests through free-to-spend: trecho-funded subtracts from the 678, new/existing pote do not touch the trecho; an existing pote debits only that pote. **M4.4** pure `isEventVisibleOnHome`/`selectVisibleEvents` (mirrors D8): a dated event surfaces on the Home only when its owner trecho is active OR within the D-7 window; the dashboard exposes `upcomingEvents` and `DashboardCards` shows them as discreet heads-up. **TripHub** the "Potes e planejados" section now also lists open events (name + date range + funding source). **i18n** pt-BR/en/es for the whole `plan.*` namespace + event chips + new `trip_hub`/`fab`/`dashboard` keys. Backend/schema tables untouched; web/OTA only; APK preserved byte-identical at the verified 0.56.0 shell.

A "Pote" is now ONE concept — money set apart with a purpose — with OPTIONAL date and goal; fund/pool/scope jargon stays in the backend. **M3.1 (D7)** `BudgetPool` gains optional `dateStart`/`dateEnd`/`goalCents` (type + `createBudgetPool` factory + Zod, all default null so every pre-v10 backup and every `linked_phases` trecho pool stays valid); **Dexie v10** additive, non-indexed upgrade backfills the three fields to null on existing pools — `totalAmountCents` is NEVER touched (money invariant unit-tested). **M3.2 (D7/master §10.4)** new guided `CreatePotSheet` (`features/trip/PotSheets.tsx`): name + amount, with date (drives D8) and goal behind toggles, example chips that prefill the name, and a "why" microcopy; reuses `createBudgetPoolWithPhaseLinks` (extended to pass date/goal). **M3.3 (D8)** pure `isPotVisibleOnHome`/`selectVisiblePots` (`domain/budget/pots.ts`): a DATED pot surfaces on the Home only when its OWNER trecho is active OR today is within the D-7 window up to its end; a DATELESS pot is ambient (always shown — no regression for pots that predate the date field). **M3.4 (D9)** new "Potes e planejados" section on `TripHubPage` lists EVERY pot (balance + date + goal-progress bar) plus the open planned purchases and a "Novo pote" door; pots were removed from the raw trecho-funds list (`contextPools` now `linked_phases` only). **D8 wiring** the dashboard exposes `visiblePotSummaries`; the Home `funds_summary` card shows only the relevant pots (the full list lives in the section); `globalPoolSummaries` stays complete for leftover-move targets. **M3.5** early spend already works (a pot is a conscious pick in QuickAdd for any active trecho — `getAvailablePoolsForPhase` returns global pots regardless of phase); added a canonical unit test (€50 spent on the Tomorrowland pot from the Burgos trecho debits ONLY the pot, the active trecho budget intact). **i18n** pt-BR/en/es for the whole `pote.*` namespace + new `trip_hub` keys. - **🟢 0.77.0 (deployed, web-only OTA) — Pacote #1 (Budget Model Reform) · GATE 2 — Trecho with a dedicated budget + vocabulary + sequential dates + total=sum COMPLETE.** Plan: `brain/documents/budget-model-implementation-prompt-2026-06-17.md` (decisions in `budget-model-master-decision-2026-06-17.md`). A "Trecho" is now a real-world concept the user creates directly (piece of the trip = dates + a budget); the fund/pool/link jargon stays in the backend. **M2.1** pure-shaped orchestrator `createPhaseWithBudget` (`crud-orchestrators.ts`) creates the `Phase` + dedicated `linked_phases` `BudgetPool` + 1:1 `BudgetPoolPhaseLink` in ONE Dexie transaction (atomic: a trecho can never exist without its budget; rollback unit-tested). **M2.2** new guided door `AddTrechoSheet` (`features/trip/TrechoSheets.tsx`) on the Viagem hub — name + dates + budget, microcopy "um trecho é um pedaço da viagem", smart default dates (day after the last trecho), no jargon. **M2.3** pure `validatePhaseSequence`/`findOverlappingPhases`/`phaseRangesOverlap` (`domain/phases/phase-sequence.ts`): D12 blocks overlaps; D13 boundary-day rule — a trecho starting on another's last day trims the previous one to the day before (the boundary belongs to the trecho that STARTS), shown as an editable amber notice. **M2.4** D14 — the Viagem total is now `computeTripBudgetTotals` (sum of trechos; pots counted apart) with a "Soma dos trechos" hint; over-budget trechos show "Passou €X · Remanejar"; `transferBetweenPools` (reuses `computePoolTransfer`) moves budget between trechos in one transaction preserving the trip total (invariant unit-tested), surfaced via `RemanejarSheet` + `summarizeTrechoBalance`. **M2.5** i18n pt-BR/en/es for the whole `trecho.*` namespace + new `trip_hub` keys. **Scope note:** the optional destructive "split an existing legacy shared pool into per-trecho pools" assistant is intentionally DEFERRED (master: "nunca forçado") — it re-points transactions/envelopes/plans and touches the money invariant, too risky to ship unsupervised; legacy trips stay 100% functional (GATE 1) and users can already add dedicated trechos additively. Backend/schema untouched; web/OTA only; APK preserved byte-identical at the verified 0.56.0 shell.

- **🟢 0.76.0 (deployed, web-only OTA) — Pacote #1 (Budget Model Reform) · GATE 1 — dashboard follows the ACTIVE phase COMPLETE.** Plan: `brain/documents/budget-model-implementation-prompt-2026-06-17.md` (decisions in `budget-model-master-decision-2026-06-17.md`). Root cause fixed: the dashboard hero + Viagem phase cards read `linkedPools[0]` (a leftover from the single-fund era), so a multi-phase trip kept showing the FIRST pool even after entering a later phase. **M1.1** pure `selectActivePhasePool(pools, links, activePhaseId)` in `domain/budget/budget.ts`: a legacy 1-pool/N-phases trip resolves to that one pool for every phase (AC1 — byte-identical), the new 1-pool-per-phase model resolves the active phase's pool, and a coherent fallback (first linked pool) covers "no active phase / no dedicated pool yet" — so the hero never blanks. **M1.2** `useDashboardModel` hero now calls the selector with `activePhase?.id`. **M1.3** `TripHubPage.freeForPhase(phaseId)` resolves each phase's OWN pool (per-card free-to-spend) instead of a fixed `primaryPool`. **M1.4** new E2E `active-phase-budget.spec.ts` (2 specs): the hero renders the active phase's money, and switching the phase chip recomputes the per-phase free-to-spend. Backend/schema untouched; web/OTA only; APK preserved byte-identical at the verified 0.56.0 shell.

Reset on attachment change; objectURL still revoked on unmount. **D-BUG-16 (photo block pushed the screen)** — new `compact` prop on `AttachmentSection`: a single horizontal row (small add-chip + thumbnails) instead of the tall `py-6` empty block; used by the active outing's `photosSlot` so the quick-add stays above the fold. - **🟠 0.69.0 (APK built, NOT promoted — device-pending) — Master Fix & Skipped-Features plan · Onda 4 (B1+B2+B3 native batch) CODE-COMPLETE, CI gates green.** Plan/diary: `brain/documents/master-fix-and-skipped-features-plan-2026-06-17.md` + `waves-execution-log-2026-06-17.md`. **DEC-215.** **B1 (receive `.csv`)** — native `ShareTargetPlugin` (`@CapacitorPlugin("ShareTarget")`) reads a shared/opened CSV (ACTION_SEND `EXTRA_STREAM` via `IntentCompat`/`EXTRA_TEXT`, or ACTION_VIEW `content`/`file`) into a static buffer; `MainActivity` processes the intent in `onCreate`/`onNewIntent`; manifest intent-filters for `text/csv` / `text/comma-separated-values` / `application/csv` (CSV-only → no generic-text hijack). JS boundary `utils/native/share-target.ts` keeps the CSV in memory; `RootLayout` (`useNativeIntents`) routes to `/import/wise?shared=1`; `WiseImportPage` drains it through the same pure `parseWiseCsv` path as a manual upload (refactored into one `ingestCsvTexts`). **B2 (App Links)** — `public/.well-known/assetlinks.json` (debug SHA-256), manifest `autoVerify` intent-filter for `https://trippilot.pages.dev/pair` + `/s/*`, `utils/native/deep-link.ts` (`parseDeepLink` pure + `initDeepLinks`) navigates preserving the `#fragment` (the `/s` key + `/pair` identity). **B3 (native GPS)** — already code-complete (`ensureLocationPermission` via `@capacitor/geolocation` + manifest perms); no change. All native paths guarded by `isNativeApp()` → web/PWA byte-unaffected (PWA Web Share Target intact).

New non-indexed settings `appLockBiometricEnabled` / `appLockBiometricCredentialId` (default off/null, backfilled).

Built **invariance-first** from the domain core out, purely additive: `calculatePoolIncome` grows the pool inside `calculateFreeToSpend` (`budget + income − …`), `calculateWalletBalance` credits the destination wallet, `createIncomeTransaction` (personalCost/category null, `excludeFromLearning: true`). `calculatePoolSpent` stays expense+adjustment only → income is **never** "spent" and **never** feeds learning. Dedicated `IncomePage` (FAB → "Registrar entrada", route `/income`) so the critical `QuickAddPage` is **untouched**. FTS breakdown shows a green additive "Entradas recebidas" line (omitted when 0); recent activity shows income in green with a savings icon. **With zero income every downstream number is bit-identical to before** (proven by 18 `income.test.ts` cases incl. FTS/wallet/summary invariance).

Web/OTA only — no worker change, no new APK (`latestNativeVersion` 0.56.0, `requiredNativeVersion` 0.50.0).
- **Native App Links (F19) — deferred, by design**: real Android App Links need (a) a manifest `autoVerify` intent-filter, (b) `/.well-known/assetlinks.json` with the **signing-cert SHA-256**, and (c) `App.addListener('appUrlOpen', …)` → router wiring (the Capacitor webview loads local assets, not the remote URL). Without a device to verify the handoff, shipping it blind risks a link that opens the app but doesn't navigate — the exact bug we agreed to avoid. So it joins the next **device-verified native batch** (same pattern as the deferred `/s/:id` App Links). The web `/pair` link is 100% functional today (browser + PWA; on native it opens the browser → PWA).
- **🟢 0.58.0 (prior) — Shared Participant Link epic COMPLETE incl. real-time (S7).** Full delivery report: `brain/documents/delivery-report-shared-link-and-realtime-2026-06-16.md`. A split can now be shared with someone who does **not** have the app via a link `…/s/:id#k=<aesKey>`: they open it, see **only their slice** (owner/mirror, DEC-106), confirm/reject lines, and mark "paguei" → the owner reconciles. Everything is **E2E-encrypted** (the AES key rides in the URL `#fragment`, never reaches the server). Storage = **Cloudflare KV (`SHARE_STORE`)** (write-token-gated owner ops; open ciphertext reads). **Real-time (S7, 0.58.0):** a `ShareSignal` Durable Object is a pure WebSocket fanout relay (transport only, stores nothing) — owner edits land on a connected guest **live** with a toast; guest confirmations/settlements land on the owner live. Honest limits: app-open only (no FCM/Play Services for app-closed push; iOS-web falls back) → **async pull is the guaranteed floor**.
- **Prior field-feedback fixes (0.56.0, still live):** **DEC-208** receipt split is an explicit participant picker (no auto-everyone); **DEC-209** on-device OCR removed (cloud Groq is the only reader — local "não leu nada certo"); dropped `@jcesarmobile/capacitor-ocr` + `tesseract.js` → **APK 21 MB → 8 MB**; **DEC-210** native APK self-update (`ApkInstaller` plugin + manifest `latestNativeVersion` + boot/Settings one-tap; build copies APK → dev Downloads).
- **Last Green Test Run**: **1096 tests pass (124 files)** — incl. a real AES-GCM owner↔guest share cycle + share-link/response domain tests; tsc clean (app + worker); web build green. **Node 22 required** (Node 18 lacks `globalThis.crypto.subtle`).
- **E2E (Playwright)**: 3 specs PASSING across isolated browser contexts — `share-link.spec.ts` (owner→guest→owner reconcile; malformed-link recoverable error; **S7 live toast with NO guest action**) + `receipt-split.spec.ts` (picker defaults to personal, DEC-208). 6 screenshots captured.
- **APK**: `dist/trippilot.apk` is the **0.56.0** native shell (versionCode 21, 8 MB). The Shared Link epic (0.57.0) + real-time (0.58.0) are **100% web/relay** (Dexie + fetch + WebSocket + pure domain) → ship **OTA only**; `latestNativeVersion` stays **0.56.0**. **Deferred to S8 (next native bump):** Android App Links for `/s/:id` (assetlinks) + a foreground `LocalNotification`.
- **Deploy**: Production apex `trippilot.pages.dev` via `--branch=master` — **0.58.0** web bundle. Verified `/version.json` 0.58.0 (`access-control-allow-origin: *` + `cache-control: no-store`), `/bundles/0.58.0.zip` (649 KB, CORS `*`), `/trippilot.apk` (200), SPA fallback for `/s/:id` + `/shared-with-me` (200 html). Worker `trippilot-sync` redeployed (KV `SHARE_STORE` + `ShareSignal` DO, migration v3) and lifecycle-verified live: create→writeToken, open read, guest `POST /responses {id,blob}`→ok, `GET /share/:id/ws`→DO (`expected_websocket`, relay bound), owner revoke→410.
- **Confidence**: 95% — domain/crypto unit-tested (real AES-GCM cycle), worker lifecycle smoke-verified in production, full owner→guest→owner + live-update proven in Playwright across two browsers. Residual: app-closed push and iOS-web real-time are intentionally out of scope (async pull floor covers them); native App Links wait for S8.

#### Field Round 2 · Wave E — Smart Wise import: bridge + person + split + create-phase + guess (0.63.0, OTA) [F16 + F16b + F16c]
Source: Julio field feedback round 2, plan `brain/documents/improvements-master-plan-2026-06-16-round2.md` — the "intelligence" Julio asked the importer to have: predict the real relationships behind a statement (a split purchase + its reimbursement) and fill the gaps (a missing phase, a special-purchase category). Locked decision: **all three** (a vínculo entrada↔compra + criar pessoa + divisão/quitação; b criar fase na importação; c adivinhar categoria de compras especiais), **always confirming**. 100% web/pure → OTA, no new deps. Sequenced E1(F16c, lowest risk) → E3(F16a) → E2(F16b) to add the money-touching path last, behind tests.
- [x] **E1 — F16c special-purchase category**: extended `CATEGORY_RULES` `entertainment` regex with ticketing/festival brands (paylogic, eventim, ticketmaster, ticketone, see tickets, ticketswap, viagogo, stubhub, eventbrite, wegow, tomorrowland, dice/fever/axs whole-word, ra.co, resident advisor, ingresse, sympla). Pure addition; distinctive brands as stems, short ambiguous tokens whole-word so they never shadow a clearer category (test: "Feverish Cafe" → restaurant). +4 `guessCategory` assertions.
- [x] **E3 — F16a reimbursement bridge (pure detector)**: new `domain/import/reimbursement-bridge.ts` `detectReimbursementBridges({ drafts, windowDays?, minScore? })` → ranks incoming-transfer ↔ expense-draft links by a transparent heuristic: amount **≤** purchase (a share can't exceed the whole), within a **±21-day** window (default), score = time-proximity (dominant) + amount plausibility, with penalties for a repayment landing **before** the purchase and for tiny slivers. **Greedy 1:1** assignment (each purchase/repayment used once). 10 detector tests (canonical Paylogic case, rejects amount>purchase, window/minScore honored, greedy pairing, full-reimburse ratio, before-penalty, exclusions of outgoing/credit/fee/duplicate).
- [x] **E3 — F16a commit (split-on-import)**: `commitWiseImport` gained optional `ownerId` + `bridges: { rowId → { participantId, shareAmountCents } }`. A bridged purchase is committed as a **shared** expense (owner paid, custom split via `resolvePayerExpense`), with the person's share **born confirmed** (the repayment lands in the same batch, so the debt is acknowledged now — without this DEC-071 would leave it pending and the settlement wouldn't net). Shares written in the same Dexie tx (additive — no-bridge path byte-identical). 3 commit tests incl. **split + `settle_incoming` → `calculateDebts` total = 0**.
- [x] **E3 — F16a UI (suggest → confirm → wire)**: `WiseImportPage` shows a **"Sugestões inteligentes"** section; each suggestion opens a `BridgeConfirmSheet` (pre-matched person via `matchParticipantByName`, create-inline reusing the transfer flow's pattern, the "Bianca devia X de Y · sua parte Z" explainer). On confirm it records the bridge **and** pre-classifies the matching incoming transfer to `settle_incoming` (with an `ignore` remainder if the repayment exceeds the share) — so the existing `commitWiseTransfers` path settles it. The expense row gets a "Dividido c/ {name}" badge; `handleCommit` forwards only bridges whose purchase is actually imported, with `ownerId`. Unlink supported.
- [x] **E2 — F16b create-phase-on-import**: new non-persisted `WiseImportDraft.inPhase` (strict `findActivePhase` — true only when the date is inside a real phase; the fallback `phaseId` still attaches the row so nothing is orphaned). The page groups out-of-phase importable rows into a banner → `CreatePhaseSheet` pre-filled with the rows' min/max date → `createPhase` + `phaseRepository.create`, then re-classifies the kept `rows` against `[...phases, newPhase]` (selection/transfer/bridge state survives — all keyed by rowId). +1 classify test (out-of-phase → `inPhase:false`, fallback `phaseId` retained).
- [x] **i18n** new `wiseImport.bridge_*` (title/hint/suggestion/linked/split_with/someone/review/sheet_title/days_apart/explainer/my_cost/confirm) + `wiseImport.out_of_phase_*`/`create_phase*`/`phase_name*`/`phase_start`/`phase_end`/`phase_created`, in pt-BR/en/es (count strings use the project's "(s)" style — no i18next v4 plural keys).

Playwright full-page visual QA on a real CSV upload (suggestions / bridge sheet / linked state with auto-set transfer + expense badge / out-of-phase banner → create-phase → banner clears). `APP_VERSION`/`package.json`/`version.json` → **0.63.0**, release note pt/en/es. `make-ota-bundle` → `bundles/0.63.0.zip` (686 KB), APK preserved (0.50.0 native).
